Current lines:
  - **Sub Health Centre** — first contact point of the primary tier
  - **PHC** — referral centre for SHCs; belongs to the **primary** tier
  - **Urban PHC** — **25,000-30,000** people within **2-3 km**

  - **HIV and AIDS (Prevention and Control) Act, 2017** — bars **discrimination** against people living with HIV

  - AP HIV status — **88%, 87% and 96.2%**; target **December 2026**

  - **Mosquirix** — first WHO-approved **malaria** vaccine; **R21** — Oxford and Serum Institute

Current lines:
  # Import dependence
  - India imports about **89%** of its oil
  - **GCC** — about **40%** of India's **urea** imports; **60%** or more of LNG imports
  - **Strait of Hormuz–Persian Gulf** — more than **85%** of India's LPG imports pass through it
  - **Indian Potash Limited** — tender for **2.5 million tonnes of urea** (2026 fertilizer shock)
  # Rupee, gold and reserves
  - PM's appeal (Hyderabad rally) — avoid **non-essential foreign travel** and **gold purchases** for **one year**
  - Revised **gold and silver import duty** — to **curb imports**, protect forex, support the rupee, **reduce CAD**
  - **Weak rupee** — causes **imported inflation**; export gains cut by import content and India's **price-taker** status
  - **Forex reserves** — foreign currency assets, gold, SDRs and **reserve tranche in the IMF**
  - **Dornbusch Overshooting Model** — exchange rates overreact in the short run because prices are **sticky**
  # Relief measures
  - **ECLGS 5.0** — approved by the **Union Cabinet**; guarantees through **NCGTC**; MSMEs **100%** coverage, non-MSMEs and aviation **90%**
  - Proposed **MSME credit guarantee** (based on ECLGS) — **90%** guarantee on loans up to **₹100 crore**
  - **Bharat Maritime Insurance Pool** — insures Indian ships on **high-risk or war-affected routes**, especially **West Asia**
  # Other
  - **UDGAM** — unclaimed deposits (unused **10 years**) go to the **Depositor Education and Awareness Fund**
  - **Sugarcane 2024–25** — **444.9 MT**; **Uttar Pradesh 48.5%**, Maharashtra 24.1%, **Karnataka 10.5%**
  - **Spices Board** — HQ **Kochi**
  - **SKO** — used in wick and pressure stoves, lamps and as a solvent (not a transport fuel)

F1559 Q: Assertion (A): Keytruda directly kills cancer cells. / Reason (R): Keytruda blocks the PD-1 receptor on T-cells and reactivates immunity. || ANSWER: A is false, but R is true || WHY: The assertion is false because Keytruda does not directly kill cancer cells.   - India's first national counter-terrorism policy — **MHA**, **23 February 2026**; **seven** pillars (incl. Prevention, Recovery and resilience)

  - **CAG audit** applies to **NDRF**, not PM CARES; SC (2020) refused transfer of PM CARES money to NDRF

  - **COP29** (**Baku**, 2024) — **US$300 billion** annual finance goal by **2035**; **Baku-to-Belém Roadmap** to **US$1.3 trillion**

  - **Tropical Forest Forever Facility** (COP30) — **Brazil-led**; **US$125 billion**; India joined as an **Observer**

  - COP30 pledges — **voluntary**, not legally binding; the **US skipped** both the 2025 G20 Summit and COP30
  - India has hosted only **COP8** (**2002**); COP26 was at **Glasgow**
  - **COP33** (2028) — India **withdrew its bid** (Environment Ministry letter, **2 April 2026**); **South Korea** the only other contender
  - **CBD COP16** — **Cali** (Oct 2024; resumed **Rome**, Feb 2025); created the **Cali Fund** (digital sequence information)
  - first CBD COP — **Nassau, Bahamas**, 1994

  - **Madhya Pradesh** — most tiger reserves (**9**), incl. **Madhav NP** (Shivpuri), India's 58th
  - **NSTR** — four female tigers from **Chandrapur** (Maharashtra) and **Madhya Pradesh**; **gaur** moved from September 2026; largest tiger reserve
  - **Tirupati** — in line for **Wetland City** status
  - Seshachalam Biosphere Reserve — 2010; **Koundinya** — only Elephant Reserve in AP
  # Species and sites
  - **Dipturus dhritiae** — **deep-sea skate**, **Arabian Sea** (Deep Ocean Mission); named after **Dhriti Banerjee**, first woman Director of ZSI
  - **Lycodon irwini** (Irwin's wolf snake) — **Great Nicobar Island**
  - **Hope Spots** — **Mission Blue** with IUCN; **GSI** — National Geo-Heritage Sites
F1690 Q: Which one of the following is NOT correctly matched? || ANSWER: Hope Spot — Geological Survey of India || WHY: Hope Spots are associated with Mission Blue, in collaboration with IUCN. The Geological Survey of India is associated with National Geo-Heritage Sites.
